# Production readiness remediation 2026-09

Status: **landed on `main` on 2026-09-23** with the whole 2026-09 program, by one squash pull request (#201, squash commit `1911559`). The per-commit history this ledger cites is kept under the tag `archive/program-2026-09`, and all ten CI jobs are required checks on `main` (After the program). Work continues on branches cut from `main`. Slice 15 — Docs + closeout — verified 2026-09-21, the last slice: every slice has landed — S0, S1, S2, S3, S4, S8, S9, S12, S13, S14 and S15 verified (S2 since 2026-09-22, when the operator signed Appendix B item 1), S5, S6, S7, S10 and S11 landed and waiting only on Appendix B items 2–5 — and the program stays open until Appendix B is signed (item 8, the operator's); on branch `production-readiness-2026-09` (cut from `ui-gold-standard-2026-09` @ `c03e1db`, so the whole 2026-09 stack is carried inside it; S0 = `d935aaa` + `cfa91e4`, S1 = `b9da9dc` + `38ffc0d`, S2 = `c2b7361` + `214bdcb`, S3 = `e4c283d` + `011fe44`, S4 = `f3db990` + `c126b10`, S5 = `f1dc897` + `c4d849f` + `eaa3cc5`, S6 = `bee067b` + `330c3fb`, S7 = `9bb1f8d` + `c74ca5f` + `1799e9b`, S8 = `b00ecaf` + `8b1892e`, S9 = `96ae4e9` + `c87565a` + `96939dc`, S10 = `957acc9` + `04b68d7`, S11 = `9c45d36` + `4b89191` + `d0ef0ff`, S12 = `d507356` + `8b8d113` + `d08a8cf`, S13 = `cf681ad` + `ab581e4` + `10d5dbf`, S14 = `354306c` + `6fbae80`, S15 = `93603e1` + `b098233` + its ledger follow-up, all pushed; seven commits in the range are not slices: `235241c`, the guard on `npm run clean`, `db2df4d` (2026-09-21), the header lamps reading the hardware link's own status words, `7b85f04` (2026-09-22), room for the deck's once-a-second LCD burst, `e991cc8`, the lane fix of the same day, the setup-support lane counting launches from numbers it waited for, `7413b53`, also of that day, the fixture double answering the rig's eight lighting actions, the fixture double's Recent actions, which list every action the screen asks for, and Setup's reading of the hardware link's own words for the Stream Deck bridge and the health checks (the five After the program)). CI runs on every branch push; the first run on the stack (34459772849) is recorded under Slice 0, the S1 run (34472807896) was the first with `qualification` green, the S2 (34487837771), S3 (34507456992), S4 (34514042594), S5 (34574043349, after the lane follow-up), S6 (34593996173), S7 (34602095638, on the baselines commit), S8 (35201156819) and S9 runs (35208750697 on the slice commit, 35209951483 on the queue follow-up) kept that shape, and the S10 run (35230069147) kept it too, and so did the S11 run (35261830154, on the baselines commit); the S12 runs kept it as well — 35281353996 on the slice commit and 35282760471 on the evidence follow-up, now with a ninth job, `supply-chain`, green on both — and the `release-evidence` workflow ran green on the rehearsal tag on both commits (35281355605, 35282765635: Windows and macOS bundles, SHA256 manifests, SBOMs; the tag deleted afterwards) — through S12 `frontend-e2e` was red on every run: the same 91 stale or missing linux baselines and five behaviour cases that turned out to be defects of the tests. S13 refreshed the baselines, fixed the five at their cause and made the job fail on its `default` project (three wall-clock cases run in a step that reports and never fails it, until 2026-10-31); the workflow has ten jobs now (`rust-coverage`), the rehearsal run of the S13 tree, 35336198990, was the first on this stack with every job green, and the S13 run on the branch (35337703363, on the baselines commit) was the second — its `frontend-e2e` job green on five consecutive executions; the S14 run (35354775196, on the slice commit) was the third, every job green on the first attempt; its ledger follow-up's run (35356465640) failed `rust-coverage` on a Rust timing test and was not re-run — Slice 15 made that pair wait on state — and the run on `db2df4d` (35594846828) was all ten jobs green on the first attempt; the Slice 15 runs are recorded under Slice 15: 35605667300 on the slice commit, nine jobs green and `format-protocol` red on a new test that needed a Storybook build the job does not make, and 35607162991 on its quarantine-test follow-up, every job green on the first attempt; the runs of the bridge fix `7b85f04` (35712843386), of its ledger follow-up (35714853611, red on `qualification`), of the lane fix `e991cc8` (35751453676) and of the fixture-double fix `7413b53` (35758670685) are recorded under After the program. The workstation baseline is under Slice 0 (with an S1 correction on `scripts:test`). Appendix B item 1 was signed on 2026-09-22 (After the program). Open operator items: Appendix B item 2 (end the engine process during a session and launch a second copy of the app; S5 moves to `verified` when it is signed), Appendix B item 3 (confirm live TotalMix meters and console echo with the loopback bind; S6 moves to `verified` when it is signed), Appendix B item 4 (verify and restore a database backup from Setup / Support and confirm fixtures, scenes, deck bindings return; S7 moves to `verified` when it is signed) and Appendix B item 5 (sACN to the real bridge starting with `SSE_SAFE_START=1`, arming from Setup / Support, the Stream Deck's lighting keys while previewing, and Recent actions showing who did what; S10 and S11 move to `verified` when it is signed). None is blocking. **Since 2026-09-21 the live app is this branch's build** (the operator's decision that day: "Move to the new build and use real data"): `release\native\windows\SSE ExEd Studio Control Native\` became the build of `6fbae80` at 12:56 and the build of `db2df4d` at 13:37 (shell 10,375,168 bytes, sha256 `6e148cd8…`; engine 9,342,464 bytes, sha256 `e3ca68a4…`), and on 2026-09-22 at 11:57 the build of `7b85f04`, the bridge fix (shell 10,375,680 bytes, sha256 `859b40ff…`; engine 9,342,976 bytes, sha256 `ecdb011d…`; After the program); the `db2df4d` build is kept at `release\native\windows.db2df4d-2026-09-21\`, the `6fbae80` build at `release\native\windows.6fbae80-2026-09-21\` and the S2-era build at `release\native\windows.s2-era-2026-09-10\`, which cannot open the data any more. It runs on the real app-data, whose database has been at schema 7 since 2026-09-18 08:27 (a run of this branch's build that morning; who launched it is not recorded) — the correction under Slice 11 has the details. Appendix B item 7 (signing certificates, D2) now names the secrets that wake the dormant signing steps; it blocks nothing. Slice 14 split the three orchestrators, the fixture double and the Rust lighting tests by line range, gave each workspace a chunk of its own and removed the file-size guard's allowlist for source files; by the operator's ruling of 2026-09-18 only the Windows build at 2560×1440 counts from here on (Slice 14, Rescope 14). Slice 15 emptied the Playwright quarantine at its causes, made the two racing Rust tests wait on state, fixed the Console's plate-head markup and brought the documents to the program's end. Next: nothing in this program but Appendix B.

Tracking: one commit per slice `Readiness S<N>: <what landed>` (baseline refreshes as `Readiness S<N> (baselines): …`), with a What / Why / Tests / Verified body. The per-slice `Status:` lines below are the execution record; any divergence from a slice's written scope gets a bold **Rescope:** paragraph under that slice per the AGENTS.md rescope protocol — no silent substitution. Status vocabulary: `planned` → `in progress (next: <step>)` → `landed (operator verification pending)` (commit named; all lanes green; hardware not yet walked) → `verified (<date>, Appendix B item N)`. Slices with no hardware exposure (S0, S1, S4, S9, S12, S13, S14, S15) go straight to `verified` when their lanes are green. Every slice records, before it closes: what the existing gates asserted before the change, the tests added that would have caught the finding, and every test that had to change (old assertion → new assertion → reason).

Goal / Sources: close all 32 findings (F01–F32) of the 2026-09-10 production readiness audit of this program (verdict NOT PRODUCTION READY: 3 critical, 16 major, 13 minor; artifact "Studio Control Readiness Audit", https://claude.ai/code/artifact/06da97b6-852a-421f-901d-6541fa233978, memory note `production-readiness-audit-2026-09-10`). Design (authoritative for _what to build_): `~/.claude/plans/i-want-you-to-logical-quilt.md` on the operator's workstation — it is not in the repository; each slice below carries the scope it needs. If this ledger and the design disagree, these `Status:` lines win for what has happened and the design wins for what to build, unless a **Rescope:** paragraph here says otherwise. Outcome: every finding closed by a change **and** a named guard (test or lane) that fails if it regresses; CI running on every push; the operator hardware checklist enumerated for sign-off; verdict re-ratable to "ready with minor changes" once Appendix B is signed.

Session protocol: start with `git status && git log --oneline -10`; read `AGENTS.md`, this file's `Status:` / `Tracking:` paragraphs and the first slice not `verified`; re-read that slice in the design; confirm the production app is closed (port 38201 free, TotalMix untouched); run only that slice's lanes to re-establish state. End by updating the slice `Status:` with `next:`, recording lane results with dates under Validation, committing only complete slices, and describing any uncommitted work in `Status:` so the next session can resume or discard it — never leave a half-slice committed. Work sequentially in one session; no parallel subagents (they exhaust this workstation's usage limits).

Hardware safety (binding for every lane in this program): tests never write to TotalMix ports 7001–7010 (`SSE_ENGINE_TEST_ALLOW_CONSOLE_WRITES` stays unset; test sockets ≥ 7011); the production app is closed before any `tauri:*:qualify` or bridge lane (ports 38201 / 4173, single instance from S5 on); Playwright and a qualification lane never run at the same time (both bind 4173); sACN toward the real bridge only in Appendix B sessions with the operator present, from S11 on starting with `SSE_SAFE_START=1`; never load a TotalMix snapshot slot; `native:acceptance` stays simulated (`SSE_NATIVE_ACCEPTANCE_LIVE_CONSOLE` unset).

Mechanical drift guards: `scripts/check-slice-rescope.mjs` (a slice title change without a **Rescope:** paragraph is refused at commit), `npm run protocol:check`, `tests/contract.rs`, `EVENT_DOMAIN_REFRESH satisfies Record<EventName, …>` (S9 on), `scripts/file-health.mjs`, the ui-contract ratchets (a measure may fall, never rise), `scripts/check-operator-copy.mjs` (never "engine", "backend", "transport", "IPC", "snapshot" outside the Console — say "hardware link", "backup", "Stream Deck profile", "light outputs", "saved data"), coverage floors (S13 on: Vitest in `dev:check`, Rust in the `rust-coverage` job), `scripts/check-playwright-quarantine.mjs` (S13 on: the Playwright quarantine list stays short, explained and dated; no gate can be put on it).

**Rescope:** none at program level; slice-level paragraphs sit under Slice 1 (four deviations), Slice 2 (four), Slice 3 (two), Slice 4 (five), Slice 5 (three), Slice 6 (none in scope, two additions recorded), Slice 7 (three), Slice 8 (seven), Slice 9 (eight), Slice 10 (ten), Slice 11 (twelve), Slice 12 (twelve), Slice 13 (thirteen), Slice 14 (fourteen) and Slice 15 (ten), each old scope → new scope → why, with the slice titles unchanged because what landed is what the titles say. A slice whose premise changes on inspection gets its own **Rescope:** paragraph under its heading and a renamed title that matches what landed.

## Locked decisions (operator, 2026-09-10)

| #                   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| D1                  | Lighting output at launch keeps today's auto-stream; the fix adds an opt-in hold (`SSE_SAFE_START=1` env + persisted `app.lighting.output_armed`, default `true`) with a Support › Workstation switch.           |
| D2                  | Release chain: tag-triggered CI builds packaged bundles, SHA256 manifests and CycloneDX SBOMs; signing steps are wired but dormant until certificate secrets exist. No certificate purchase in this program.     |
| D3                  | F26 (splitting the three large orchestrators) is the last implementation slice, guarded by ui-contract, visual-review and Playwright.                                                                            |
| D4                  | Slice 0 changes the CI trigger only; the operator opens pull requests and sets branch protection themselves.                                                                                                     |
| D5 (design default) | Bridge token is generated per install, stored in app-data, and embedded in the exported Companion profile; the operator re-imports the profile once (Full Reset & Import).                                       |
| D6 (design default) | No new UI surfaces beyond: recovery copy for new failure codes, a Verify-backup action, an Outputs Armed/Held switch, a Recent-actions list in Support. Everything else is engine/shell/store work. No redesign. |

## Slice 0 — Branch, ledger, CI on every push, baseline

Status: verified 2026-09-10 (commit `d935aaa`, pushed; every `dev:check` step green on the workstation; the push produced the first CI run ever on this stack, recorded under "CI (result)" below; no hardware exposure). The CI record landed as the follow-up commit `Readiness S0 (ledger): first CI run on the branch recorded`, because a run cannot be recorded by the commit that triggers it.

Scope: branch `production-readiness-2026-09` cut from `ui-gold-standard-2026-09` @ `c03e1db`; this ledger; `.github/workflows/dev-checks.yml` `on.push.branches` `[main]` → `["**", "!dependabot/**"]` with `pull_request` and the `concurrency` block kept — the Dependabot exclusion is added to the design's `["**"]` because Dependabot branches already get a `pull_request` run and would otherwise run every job twice; `CHANGELOG.md` `[Unreleased]` carries the program placeholder paragraph and a Changed bullet for the trigger; `docs/HANDOFF.md` gets the pointer to this ledger and its two sentences that stated the old trigger are corrected (blocker 9 item 1 and the Validation Baseline paragraph), as is the one in `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md` §Pull Request CI. No source file changed.
Gates before: no CI job had ever run on `studio-bringup-sacn-globalosc`, `audit-remediation-2026-09` or `ui-gold-standard-2026-09` (64 commits ahead of `main`); every gate in the two 2026-09 ledgers was run on the studio workstation only (win32).
Tests added: none — a trigger has no unit test; its guard is the run itself (Appendix A, F03). Tests changed: none.
Validation (workstation, 2026-09-10): `npm run dev:check` green end to end (exit 0; every cache warm, so the whole bundle took about two minutes) — per-step results under Baselines. This file contains a **Rescope:** paragraph, so `scripts/check-slice-rescope.mjs` accepts its sixteen new slice titles at commit (lint-staged runs it on every staged `docs/plans/**.md`).
Baselines (workstation, 2026-09-10, tree = `c03e1db` + the S0 docs and trigger change): `format:check` clean; `lint` clean; `scripts:test` ran **0 tests** — no `scripts/**/*.test.mjs` exists yet, so the lane is empty rather than red (S12 adds the first one and must confirm the single-quoted glob in the `scripts:test` script expands under Windows `cmd`, which does not strip quotes) — corrected in S1: thirteen such files existed and CI had been running them; the workstation ran none because `cmd.exe` handed Node the single quotes literally, and the glob is double-quoted since S1 (85 tests run here); `file:health` passed with four allowed oversized sources (`frontend/app/src/app/lighting/LightingWorkspace.tsx` 3171 lines, `frontend/packages/engine-client/src/transports/fixtureTransport.ts` 6659, `native/rust-engine/src/lighting/tests.rs` 3481, `native/rust-engine/src/rme_totalmix_osc.rs` 2893 — S14 owns the first; the other three are not in this program's scope) and one allowed large artifact; `rust:fmt:check` clean; `rust:clippy` clean under `-D warnings`; `protocol:check` clean (it prints a Node `DEP0190` deprecation warning from a `shell: true` child spawn — noise, no owning slice); `frontend:typecheck` clean across the six workspaces (27 fixture scenarios validated; token artifacts carry the A families); `frontend:test` 253 passed (app 59 in 9 files, design-system 175 in 24, engine-client 4 in 2, tokens 15 in 1, shared-graphics has no tests); `native:check` clean; `native:test` 300 passed, 1 ignored (tauri-shell 12, engine 282 + 1 ignored hardware-bound test, `contract.rs` 4, `end_to_end.rs` 2, protocol crate 0). Visual baselines were not run (no UI change); the repository carries 93 win32 / 75 linux / 75 darwin captures as HANDOFF blocker 9 records.
CI (result, 2026-09-10): run 34459772849 (https://github.com/Fikarn/sse-exed-studio-control/actions/runs/34459772849), event `push`, sha `d935aaa`, created within five seconds of the push — the first run ever on the 65-commit stack; it finished in eleven minutes. Green: `lint`, `format-protocol`, `frontend-typecheck`, `frontend-test`, `rust` (rustfmt, clippy, check, test, `native:acceptance`), `tauri-foundation`. Red, expected: `frontend-e2e` — 92 failed / 323 passed in 8.5 min: 73 linux screenshot mismatches + 18 missing linux snapshots (the stale-baseline case F03 states; the run's `playwright-test-results` artifact is the refresh source, so no pull request is needed for it — the pull request itself and the required-checks list stay the operator's, D4, Appendix B item 6) + 1 timing case, `tests/audio-arm-countdown.spec.ts:45` (on the runner the second click landed after the 350 ms arm dwell, so the recall applied; a quarantine candidate for S13, not chased here). Red, not expected: `qualification` — the setup-support qualification passed; the workspace qualification failed at `scripts/tauri-workspace-qualification.mjs:386` (`updateCommissioning-3`) with `Publish refused: Control Surface Probe not run, Audio OSC Probe failed`: the script publishes `stage: "ready"` without `overrideProbes: true` while CI sets `SSE_TAURI_QUALIFICATION_SKIP_AUDIO_PROBE=1`; the setup-support script carries the override (line 301), the workspaces script never did, and the audit-remediation Slice 8 publish gate had never run on Linux (that ledger says "scripts carry `overrideProbes`" — only one does). Also logged there: the lighting probe server cannot bind 127.0.0.1:80 on the runner (EACCES; the script continues). Owning slice: **S1** — a script-only fix recorded as a **Rescope:** under Slice 1; the job stays red at that step on every push until it lands. Not fixed in S0 (D4: trigger only).
Operator hands: none for S0. Program-level operator items are Appendix B items 6 (the PR + required checks, after S13) and 7 (signing certificates, D2).
Workstation notes for later slices (recorded once, here): a stale `vite preview --host 127.0.0.1 --port 4173 --strictPort` from an earlier session (PID 213120) was found listening on 4173; it is not the production app and serves `frontend/app/dist` from disk. Stop it before the first lane that binds 4173 (S1's `tauri:setup-support:qualify`). TotalMix FX and Companion were running throughout and were not touched; nothing listened on 38201.

## Slice 1 — Dev-fixture gate, default dirs/hosts

Status: verified 2026-09-10 (commit `b9da9dc` `Readiness S1: dev-fixture gate, platform app-data default, staged auto-import, loopback dev server`, pushed; every S1 lane and `npm run dev:check` green on the workstation; CI run 34472807896 recorded under "CI (result)" below by the follow-up commit `Readiness S1 (ledger): CI run on the branch recorded`, as in S0; no hardware exposure). Closes F04 F22 F23 F24. Depends on S0.

Scope (as landed): `native/rust-engine/Cargo.toml` gains `[features] dev-fixtures = []`. `E/main.rs` compiles `mod parity_fixtures` only under the feature; `E/app.rs` keeps `"dev.parityFixture.load" =>` as two literal one-line arms (`#[cfg(feature = "dev-fixtures")]` → today's handler, `#[cfg(not(feature = "dev-fixtures"))]` → `METHOD_UNAVAILABLE`, no events, nothing written) and gates `dispatch_parity_fixture` / `reply_with_app_commissioning_and_planning_change` with it; the method stays in `v1.contract.json` (`tests/contract.rs` green in both builds). `E/parity_fixtures.rs::parse_parity_fixture_request` defaults `replaceExistingData` to `false` (merge; `import_legacy_db` refuses the merge over existing data as `INVALID_PARAMS`). `E/bootstrap.rs`: `resolve_runtime_paths()` returns `Result<RuntimePaths, String>`; with no `SSE_APP_DATA_DIR` it resolves the platform durable directory through a copy of the shell's `default_app_data_dir_for_platform` (`%APPDATA%` → `%LOCALAPPDATA%`; `~/Library/Application Support`; `$XDG_DATA_HOME` → `~/.local/share`; joined with `ExEd Studio Control Native`, the shell's name), and with no base it fails — `main.rs` emits `engine.startupFailed {stage: "bootstrap", code: "BOOTSTRAP_FAILED"}` instead of guessing (the shell always passes the directory, so only a bare engine launch reaches this); `resolve_legacy_import_source(app_data_dir)` honours `SSE_DISABLE_AUTO_IMPORT`, then `SSE_LEGACY_DB_PATH`, then `<app-data>/import/db.json` — the working-directory scan is gone. `frontend/app/vite.config.ts` `server.host: "127.0.0.1"`. `package.json`: new `native:engine:build:dev-fixtures` (`cd native && cargo build --package studio-control-engine --features dev-fixtures`; no lane needs it — nothing calls the method — and `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md` says so next to `tauri:cutover:candidate`); `scripts:test` glob double-quoted. New `scripts/frontend/dev-server-host.test.mjs` (the F24 guard). `scripts/tauri-workspace-qualification.mjs` runs the control-surface probe too and publishes with `overrideProbes: true` only when a probe cannot pass on the host. Docs: `native/protocol/v1.md` (feature, `replaceExistingData`, import sources, app-data default), `native/README.md`, `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`, `scripts/README.md`; CHANGELOG four Changed and two Fixed bullets.
**Rescope:** four deviations from the written scope, none silent. (1) The workspace-qualification fix S0 queued here turned out wider than "add the override for CI": the lane never ran the control-surface probe, and the publish gate counts a probe that was not run as failing, so the publish was refused on every host since the gate landed (the audit-remediation Slice 8 entry says "the three publishing scripts pass `overrideProbes`" — this lane was the fourth). Landed: all three probes run, and the override is sent only when `SSE_TAURI_QUALIFICATION_SKIP_AUDIO_PROBE=1` (CI, no TotalMix) or the lighting probe server could not bind 127.0.0.1:80; on the workstation the publish therefore goes through the real gate — the evidence records `commissioning-probes-and-publish-complete.overrideProbes: false`. (2) The S0 baseline said `scripts:test` ran 0 tests because no `scripts/**/*.test.mjs` existed. Wrong: thirteen existed (eight beside their scripts, five under `scripts/release/`). The lane ran none on the workstation because npm runs scripts through `cmd.exe`, which hands Node the single-quoted glob literally (CI's bash strips the quotes, so CI ran them all along). The F24 guard lives in that lane, so the glob is double-quoted here (85 tests now run on the workstation, 81 passed, 4 skipped) and S12's "confirm the glob" note is closed. (3) The planned `bootstrap::tests::auto_import_ignores_cwd` is `tests/end_to_end.rs::auto_import_ignores_cwd`: the working directory is process-wide, so the honest test spawns the engine with a `data/db.json` under its working directory and asserts `planningProjectCount` stays 0; `bootstrap::tests` holds the pure resolver tests (`legacy_import_source_*`), and the positive twin `auto_import_reads_the_staged_app_data_file` proves the new `<app-data>/import/db.json` source imports (two projects from `fixtures/commissioning-sample-db.json`). (4) The feature gate had to extend to `E/lighting/legacy_import.rs` (`import_legacy_lighting_fixture` and its wire structs have no other caller) and to the `confidence_setting` / `ConsoleConfidence` re-export in `E/audio/mod.rs` (dead under `-D warnings` once `parity_fixtures` is compiled out; the audio writers use `helpers::` directly), and `resolve_runtime_paths` became fallible because the copied shell resolver is — the design did not spell either out; both sit inside the findings' intent.
Gates before: `tests/contract.rs` asserted only that a dispatch arm exists; nothing asserted the method's availability per build, the `replaceExistingData` default, the app-data default, the auto-import sources or the dev-server host; `scripts:test` was silently empty on the workstation.
Tests added: `parity_fixtures::tests::load_defaults_to_merge`, `load_honours_an_explicit_replace_flag` (feature build); `app::tests::parity_fixture_unavailable_without_feature` (release build — answers `METHOD_UNAVAILABLE`, no events, no fixture file written) and `parity_fixture_loads_with_feature` (feature build); `bootstrap::tests::default_app_data_dir_is_absolute_platform_dir`, `every_platform_default_mirrors_the_shell_layout`, `runtime_paths_refuse_to_guess_without_a_platform_base`, `runtime_paths_honour_explicit_overrides`, `legacy_import_source_prefers_the_explicit_path`, `legacy_import_source_reads_only_the_staged_app_data_file`, `legacy_import_source_is_disabled_by_env`; `tests/end_to_end.rs::auto_import_ignores_cwd`, `auto_import_reads_the_staged_app_data_file`; `scripts/frontend/dev-server-host.test.mjs` (three cases: Vite binds loopback; no server surface — Vite, Playwright, Storybook, Tauri dev, the two `package.json` — binds `0.0.0.0`; no helper script under `scripts/` does). Tests changed (old → new → why): `tests/end_to_end.rs::EngineProcess::spawn` → delegates to the new `spawn_with(label, configure)` with the same env and pipes (`SSE_DISABLE_AUTO_IMPORT=1` kept) → the two auto-import tests need a working directory and a staged file before the process starts; no assertion changed.
Lanes: `npm run rust:clippy`, `npm run native:test`, `cd native && cargo test -p studio-control-engine --features dev-fixtures`, `npm run protocol:check`, `npm run scripts:test`, `npm run tauri:setup-support:qualify` (proves `commissioning.seedPlanningDemo` still works), `npm run tauri:workspaces:qualify` (Rescope 1). Done when the release engine answers `METHOD_UNAVAILABLE`, the feature build passes, and `rg "0\.0\.0\.0" frontend scripts` hits only documented cases.
Validation (workstation, 2026-09-10): `cargo fmt` applied; `rust:clippy` clean under `-D warnings`, and `cargo clippy -p studio-control-engine --all-targets --features dev-fixtures -- -D warnings` clean; `native:test` 310 passed / 1 ignored (tauri-shell 12, engine 290 + 1 ignored hardware-bound, `contract.rs` 4, `end_to_end.rs` 4 — was 300 / 1); `cargo test -p studio-control-engine --features dev-fixtures` 292 + 4 + 4 passed; `protocol:check` clean (contract unchanged); `scripts:test` 85 tests, 81 passed, 4 skipped (0 ran on this workstation before); `lint`, `frontend:typecheck` (27 fixture scenarios), `format:check`, `file:health` clean; `node scripts/check-operator-copy.mjs` 0 hits; the stale `vite preview` on 4173 (PID 213120, started 2026-09-07) was stopped first; `tauri:setup-support:qualify` passed (31 s); `tauri:workspaces:qualify` passed (23 s) with the publish through the real gate (`overrideProbes: false` in the evidence; the audio probe verified against live TotalMix metering, the lighting probe server bound 127.0.0.1:80); `npm run dev:check` green end to end (65 s, warm caches). `rg "0\.0\.0\.0" frontend scripts` hits only `frontend/app/tests/setup.spec.ts` and the fixture transport's unreachable-bridge address (both documented in the guard) plus the guard itself. TotalMix FX and Companion untouched; nothing listened on 38201.
Baselines: no operator-visible change; the visual lanes were not run.
Operator hands: none.
